\bigl\|y_{\lambda_{2},\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\lambda_{1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\bigr\|_{y_{\lambda_{1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\leq\frac{2\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\left|\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}}-\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\right|+\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2},

for some

\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\leq\frac{2(\lambda_{1}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta})}{\lambda_{1}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}},\qquad\text{or}\qquad\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\leq\frac{3\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

The latter simplified bound follows because the admissible multiplier range gives

\lambda_{1}\geq 2\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}/\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
, while

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}
; hence

\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}/\lambda_{1}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}/2
.

In addition, note that

y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)=\lim_{\lambda\to\infty}y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x).

Taking
x_{1}=x_{2}=x
,
\lambda_{2}=\lambda
, and letting

\lambda_{1}\to\infty
in the bound above, using continuity of the barrier Hessian at the limiting
anchor, gives that for any
x\in X
and any finite
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
,

\bigl\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\leq\frac{2\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\lambda\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

(5)

∎

D.2
Exact Minimizer-map Stability

Proof.

We obtain the exact minimizer stability as the limiting case of the proxy minimizer stability.
Apply Proposition
2
(i) with

\lambda_{1}=\lambda_{2}=\lambda
. For every
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
,

\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\leq\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

By the proxy–exact proximity bound,

y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{i})\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{i})
as
\lambda\to\infty
for
i=1,2
.
Since the anchors remain in the interior and the barrier Hessian is continuous on
\operatorname{int}(Y)
,
we also have

H_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})=\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\to\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))=H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}).

Therefore,

\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\to\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}.

Passing to the limit in the proxy stability bound yields

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\leq\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

Thus Proposition
2
(ii) holds with

\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}:=\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}
, after increasing the constant if necessary.
∎

D.3
Local Smoothness of the Barrier-Smoothed Outer Objective

Proof.

Since
\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))=0
and
\psi_{\mu}
is strongly convex in
y
, the
implicit function theorem applies and yields

Dy_{\mu}^{\star}(x)=-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)).

By the chain rule,

\displaystyle\nabla F_{\mu}(x)

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}f(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))+\bigl(Dy_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\bigr)^{\top}\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}f(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))-\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)).

(6)

By Proposition
2
(ii) and the Euclidean–Dikin norm conversion,
after enlarging
\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}
if necessary, we have

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{2}\leq\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

(7)

From (
6
),

\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{2})-\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{1})=\underbrace{\Bigl(\nabla_{x}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{x}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr)}_{(I)}

-\underbrace{\Bigl(\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr)}_{\text{first implicit term}}

+\underbrace{\Bigl(\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr)}_{\text{second implicit term}}.

Equivalently, writing the difference of the two implicit terms as
(II)
, we have

\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{2})-\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{1})=(I)-(II).

Term
(I)
.

By the Hessian bound for
f
in Assumption
1
,

\|(I)\|_{2}\leq\ell_{f,1}\Bigl(\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}+\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{2}\Bigr)\leq\ell_{f,1}\left(1+\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\right)\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

With a slight abuse of notation, we absorb
1
into
\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}
to obtain

\|(I)\|_{2}\leq\ell_{f,1}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

Term
(II)
.

Add and subtract intermediate terms:

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle=\Bigl(\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr)^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))^{\top}\Bigl(\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigl(\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr)

\displaystyle=:\ (IIa)+(IIb)+(IIc).

We will use the following duality consequence of the cross-regularity bound in
Proposition
1
: for any

(x,y)\in X\times T_{\eta,\mu}(x)
and any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{y}}
,

\|\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y)^{\top}v\|_{2}\leq\|\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x,y)\|_{2\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x),*}\,\|v\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\|v\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}.

(8)

Bound for
(IIa)
.

By duality in the anchored Dikin geometry at
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})
,

\|(IIa)\|_{2}\leq\Bigl\|\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr\|_{2\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}

\qquad\qquad\cdot\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}.

Using cross-Hessian Lipschitzness along the minimizer graph in
Proposition
1
,

\Bigl\|\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr\|_{2\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}\leq\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

It remains to bound

\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}.

By local Dikin curvature and anchor-switch equivalence,

\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\|\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}),*}\leq\frac{\ell^{\eta}_{f,0}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

Therefore,

\|(IIa)\|_{2}\leq\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

(9)

Bound for
(IIb)
.

We have

(IIb)=\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))^{\top}\Bigl(\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})).

Applying (
8
) at

(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))
yields

\|(IIb)\|_{2}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\Bigl\|\Bigl(\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}.

Using the resolvent identity,

\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}

=\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))-\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr)\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}.

Therefore,

\Bigl\|\Bigl(\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}-\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr)\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}

\leq\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\leftarrow y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}

\quad\cdot\Bigl\|\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))-\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}

\quad\cdot\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}.

By local Dikin curvature and anchor-switch,

\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\leftarrow y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}},

and

\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\|\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}\leq\frac{\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

Finally, by the local Hessian Lipschitzness and the drift bound
(
7
),

\Bigl\|\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))-\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*\to y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}

\leq\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\Bigl(\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}+\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}\Bigr)\leq\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

Putting these together gives

\|(IIb)\|_{2}\leq\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

(10)

Bound for
(IIc)
.

We have

(IIc)=\nabla^{2}_{xy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))^{\top}\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigl(\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr).

Applying (
8
) at

(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))
gives

\|(IIc)\|_{2}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\Bigl\|\bigl(\nabla^{2}_{yy}\psi_{\mu}(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\bigr)^{-1}\Bigl(\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr)\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})}

\leq\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\Bigl\|\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}.

Using joint smoothness of
f
in
(x,y)
,

\Bigl\|\nabla_{y}f(x_{2},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2}))-\nabla_{y}f(x_{1},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}))\Bigr\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1}),*}\leq\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\Bigl(\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}+\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{2}\Bigr).

Then (
7
) yields

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{2})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{1})\|_{2}\leq\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

Therefore,

\|(IIc)\|_{2}\leq\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2}.

(11)

Combining the bounds for
(I)
, (
9
),
(
10
), and (
11
), we obtain

\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{2})-\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{1})\|_{2}\leq L_{F}^{\eta}\|x_{2}-x_{1}\|_{2},

where an explicit admissible choice is

L_{F}^{\eta}:=\ell_{f,1}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}.

(12)

Using the fact
\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}/\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\geq 1
, we get the simpler sufficient bound

L_{F}^{\eta}:=\left(\ell_{f,1}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}+2\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\,\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\right)\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}.

(13)

∎

D.4
Local Proxy-gradient Bias

Proof.

By definition,

C_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)=\min_{y\in\operatorname{int}(Y)}\Bigl(f(x,y)+\lambda\psi_{\mu}(x,y)\Bigr)-\lambda\psi_{\mu}^{\star}(x),\qquad\psi_{\mu}^{\star}(x):=\min_{y\in\operatorname{int}(Y)}\psi_{\mu}(x,y).

By the envelope theorem for the first minimization, since

\nabla_{y}(f+\lambda\psi_{\mu})(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))=0
,

\nabla_{x}\min_{y\in\operatorname{int}(Y)}\bigl(f(x,y)+\lambda\psi_{\mu}(x,y)\bigr)=\nabla_{x}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))+\lambda\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)).

For the second term, since
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)
minimizes
\psi_{\mu}(x,\cdot)
, the envelope theorem gives

\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}^{\star}(x)=\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)).

Subtracting yields

\nabla C_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)=\nabla_{x}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))+\lambda\Bigl(\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))\Bigr).

(14)

We then apply Lemma
11
with
y=y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)
. Because

y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)
minimizes
L_{\lambda,\mu}(x,\cdot)
, we have

\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda,\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))=0,

so the correction term in Lemma
11
vanishes. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x)-\nabla_{x}L_{\lambda,\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))\|_{2}

(15)

\displaystyle\leq 2\Bigl(\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\Bigr)\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\Bigl(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\lambda\min\{2\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},\,\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\}\Bigr).

By (
14
),

\nabla C_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)=\nabla_{x}L_{\lambda,\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)),

so (
15
) is a bound on

\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x)-\nabla C_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{2}
.

We then bound

\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}
by
O(1/\lambda)
.
The optimality conditions are

\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))=0,\qquad\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))+\lambda\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))=0.

Using strong monotonicity of
\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,\cdot)
on the neighborhood in the anchored Dikin geometry
(Proposition
1
), we have

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}^{2}\leq\big\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))-\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)),\,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\big\rangle.

Since
\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x))=0
, this becomes

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}^{2}\leq\big\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)),\,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\big\rangle.

Substitute

\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))=-\frac{1}{\lambda}\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))

and apply Cauchy–Schwarz in the anchored Dikin primal/dual pair:

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\|\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x),*}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}.

Canceling one factor gives

\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\|\nabla_{y}f(x,y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x))\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x),*}.

By Assumption
1
,
\|\nabla_{y}f(x,y)\|_{2}\leq\ell_{f,0}
, and by the
Euclidean–Dikin norm conversion, after absorbing the geometric conversion factor into

\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}
,

\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\leq\frac{\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\lambda\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

(16)

We then remove the
\lambda
-dependence from the bracket in
(
15
).
Using
\min\{a,b\}\leq b
and (
16
),

\lambda\min\Bigl\{2\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},\,\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\Bigr\}\leq\lambda\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\|y_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x)}\leq\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

Thus the bracket in (
15
) is bounded by the local Dikin constant

\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}.

Plugging this bound and (
16
) into
(
15
) yields

\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x)-\nabla C_{\lambda,\mu}^{\star}(x)\|_{2}\leq 2\Bigl(\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\Bigr)\cdot\frac{\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\lambda}\cdot\Bigl(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\Bigr)=\frac{c_{x}^{\eta}}{\lambda},

where an explicit admissible constant is

c_{x}^{\eta}:=2\Bigl(\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\Bigr)\Bigl(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\frac{\ell_{\psi,2}^{\eta}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\Bigr).

(17)

This proves Proposition
2
(iv).
∎

Appendix E
Tracker Contraction

We now analyze the two inner trackers under the local Dikin geometry. The tracker argument has two
layers. First, with
x_{k}
and
\lambda_{k}
fixed, the exact and proxy lower objectives are locally
well conditioned on their target neighborhoods, so repeated inner steps contract toward the corresponding
fixed centers. Second, after the outer update, the centers themselves move:

y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
changes with
x_{k}
, while

y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
changes with both
x_{k}
and
\lambda_{k}
. The moving-center
recursions transfer the fixed-center contraction from the old anchors to the new anchors and produce
the drift terms controlled by the
barrier-aware
schedule.

There is one technical point before the tracker proofs. Algorithm
1
uses the
implementable frozen barrier-metrics
\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}),\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})

during the
T
inner steps of outer iteration
k
. The analysis, however, measures tracker errors in
the unknown center-anchor metrics

H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})=\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})),\qquad H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})=\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})).

The next subsection justifies this reduction. The point is that, once the warm starts lie in the target
neighborhoods, the frozen metrics are spectrally equivalent to the corresponding center anchors by
self-concordant metric change. Moreover, a frozen-metric step has the same contraction form in the
center-anchor norm, up to fixed
\eta
-dependent constants. After this reduction, all later tracker
proofs can be written directly in the clean center-anchor Dikin norms.

E.1
Frozen Metrics and Center-anchor Reduction

Proposition 3

(Frozen Dikin metrics are center-anchor equivalent)
.

Fix an outer iteration
k
. Suppose

z_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
and

y_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
,
then

(1-\eta)^{2}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),

and

(1-\eta)^{2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

Consequently, after replacing the local Dikin constants by fixed
\eta
-dependent multiples, the
exact and proxy curvature, smoothness, and Lipschitz-Hessian bounds of
Proposition
1
also hold with respect to the frozen metrics

\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})
and
\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})
. In particular, on the exact buffer neighborhood,

\bar{\rho}_{\psi}^{\eta}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\bar{\ell}_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}),

and on the proxy buffer neighborhood,

\frac{\lambda_{k}\bar{\rho}_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\bar{\ell}_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\bar{\ell}_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}),

where one may take

\bar{\rho}_{\psi}^{\eta}:=(1-\eta)^{2}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta},\qquad\bar{\ell}_{\psi,1}^{\eta}:=(1-\eta)^{-2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},\qquad\bar{\ell}_{f,1}^{\eta}:=(1-\eta)^{-2}\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}.

Proof.

We first prove the exact-tracker metric comparison. Since

z_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, by definition

\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\eta<1.

Applying the self-concordant metric-change lemma with

y_{1}=y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
and
y_{2}=z_{k}
gives

(1-\eta)^{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\preceq\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})).

Since
H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})=\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))

, this proves

(1-\eta)^{2}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

The proxy-tracker comparison is identical. Since

y_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
,

\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\eta.

Applying the same metric-change lemma with

y_{1}=y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
and
y_{2}=y_{k}
gives

(1-\eta)^{2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

We now transfer the curvature bounds. On the exact buffer neighborhood,
Proposition
1
gives

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

From the metric comparison above,

H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}),\qquad H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\succeq(1-\eta)^{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}).

Therefore

(1-\eta)^{2}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}).

Thus the exact lower objective is strongly convex and smooth in the frozen metric
\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})
, with
constants
\bar{\rho}_{\psi}^{\eta}=(1-\eta)^{2}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
and

\bar{\ell}_{\psi,1}^{\eta}=(1-\eta)^{-2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}
.

For the proxy tracker, Lemma
3
gives, on

T_{2\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
,

\frac{\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

Using

H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\succeq(1-\eta)^{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}),\qquad H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}),

we obtain

\frac{\lambda_{k}(1-\eta)^{2}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}).

Equivalently,

\frac{\lambda_{k}\bar{\rho}_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\bar{\ell}_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\bar{\ell}_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}),

with

\bar{\ell}_{f,1}^{\eta}=(1-\eta)^{-2}\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}
.

Finally, the same metric comparison transfers the corresponding operator-norm bounds. Indeed, if

H
denotes either center anchor and
B
the corresponding frozen metric, then

(1-\eta)^{2}H\preceq B\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H

implies

\|u\|_{B}\leq(1-\eta)^{-1}\|u\|_{H},\qquad\|u\|_{H}\leq(1-\eta)^{-1}\|u\|_{B},

and

\|w\|_{B,*}\leq(1-\eta)^{-1}\|w\|_{H,*},\qquad\|w\|_{H,*}\leq(1-\eta)^{-1}\|w\|_{B,*}.

Hence any bound of the form

\|M\|_{2\to H,*}\leq C

transfers to

\|M\|_{2\to B,*}\leq(1-\eta)^{-1}C,

and any bound of the form

\|M\|_{H\to H,*}\leq C

transfers to

\|M\|_{B\to B,*}\leq(1-\eta)^{-2}C.

Similarly, Lipschitz-Hessian bounds transfer after multiplying by a fixed power of

(1-\eta)^{-1}
. Since
\eta\in(0,1/2)
is fixed, these factors are finite and can be absorbed into
the local Dikin constants. This proves the claimed frozen-metric regularity bounds.
∎

Lemma 4

(Frozen-metric steps in anchored normal form)
.

Let
c\in\operatorname{int}(Y)
, let
H_{c}:=\nabla^{2}\phi(c)
, and let

\bar{y}\in\operatorname{int}(Y)
satisfy

\|\bar{y}-c\|_{c}\leq\eta
, where
\eta\in(0,1/2)
. Define the frozen barrier-metric

B:=\nabla^{2}\phi(\bar{y})
. Let
h
be twice differentiable on the Dikin buffer

T_{2\eta}(c):=\{y\in\operatorname{int}(Y):\|y-c\|_{c}\leq 2\eta\}
, and suppose

\nabla h(c)=0
and
mH_{c}\preceq\nabla^{2}h(y)\preceq LH_{c},y\in T_{2\eta}(c),

for some
0<m\leq L<\infty
.

Let
\theta_{\eta}:=(1-\eta)^{-2}-1.
Choose
\eta
so that
\theta_{\eta}L\leq m/4
. Then, for every
y\in T_{\eta}(c)
, the frozen-metric step

y^{+}:=y-\alpha B^{-1}\nabla h(y)

satisfies

\|y^{+}-c\|_{c}^{2}\leq(1-\alpha m)\|y-c\|_{c}^{2}

whenever
\alpha(1+\theta_{\eta})^{2}L\leq\frac{1}{2}.

In particular, after taking
\eta
sufficiently small and absorbing fixed
\eta
-dependent constants
into
m
and
L
, the frozen-metric step has the same anchored contraction form as the ideal
center-metric step.

Proof.

By the self-concordant metric-change inequality and the assumption

\|\bar{y}-c\|_{c}\leq\eta
, we have

(1-\eta)^{2}H_{c}\preceq B\preceq(1-\eta)^{-2}H_{c}.

Define

S:=H_{c}^{1/2}B^{-1}H_{c}^{1/2}.

Then the eigenvalues of
S
lie in

[(1-\eta)^{2},(1-\eta)^{-2}]
. Hence

\|S\|_{2}\leq(1-\eta)^{-2}=1+\theta_{\eta},\qquad\|S-I\|_{2}\leq\theta_{\eta}.

Let

e:=y-c,\qquad p:=H_{c}^{1/2}e,\qquad r:=H_{c}^{-1/2}\nabla h(y).

The frozen-metric update can be written in the
H_{c}
-coordinates as

p^{+}:=H_{c}^{1/2}(y^{+}-c)=p-\alpha Sr.

Therefore

\|p^{+}\|_{2}^{2}=\|p\|_{2}^{2}-2\alpha\langle Sr,p\rangle+\alpha^{2}\|Sr\|_{2}^{2}.

Since
\nabla h(c)=0
and
mH_{c}\preceq\nabla^{2}h\preceq LH_{c}
on the buffer neighborhood,
h
is

m
-strongly convex and
L
-smooth in the anchored norm. Thus

\langle r,p\rangle=\langle\nabla h(y),y-c\rangle\geq m\|p\|_{2}^{2},

and

\|r\|_{2}=\|\nabla h(y)\|_{c,*}\leq L\|y-c\|_{c}=L\|p\|_{2}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\langle Sr,p\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle r,p\rangle+\langle(S-I)r,p\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\langle r,p\rangle-\theta_{\eta}\|r\|_{2}\|p\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\langle r,p\rangle-\theta_{\eta}L\|p\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-\frac{\theta_{\eta}L}{m}\right)\langle r,p\rangle\geq\frac{3}{4}\langle r,p\rangle,

where the last step uses
\theta_{\eta}L\leq m/4
.

Moreover, the
L
-smoothness and convexity of
h
in the anchored metric imply the
Baillon–Haddad inequality

\|r\|_{2}^{2}=\|\nabla h(y)\|_{c,*}^{2}\leq L\langle\nabla h(y),y-c\rangle=L\langle r,p\rangle.

Hence

\|Sr\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+\theta_{\eta})^{2}\|r\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+\theta_{\eta})^{2}L\langle r,p\rangle.

Using
\alpha(1+\theta_{\eta})^{2}L\leq 1/2
, we obtain

\alpha^{2}\|Sr\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{\alpha}{2}\langle r,p\rangle.

Combining the preceding estimates gives

\displaystyle\|p^{+}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|p\|_{2}^{2}-2\alpha\cdot\frac{3}{4}\langle r,p\rangle+\frac{\alpha}{2}\langle r,p\rangle

\displaystyle=\|p\|_{2}^{2}-\alpha\langle r,p\rangle

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha m)\|p\|_{2}^{2}.

Since
\|p\|_{2}=\|y-c\|_{c}
and
\|p^{+}\|_{2}=\|y^{+}-c\|_{c}
, this proves

\|y^{+}-c\|_{c}^{2}\leq(1-\alpha m)\|y-c\|_{c}^{2}.

∎

Throughout the algorithmic analysis, we choose
\eta
small enough so that the frozen-metric
normal-form condition in Lemma
4
holds for both the
exact and proxy tracker curvature pairs; equivalently, the fixed
\eta
-dependent metric-comparison
factors are absorbed into the local Dikin constants used in Definition
1
.

Convention for the remaining tracker analysis.

Proposition
3
and
Lemma
4
allow us to analyze the implementable frozen
barrier-metric updates as center-anchor Dikin contractions. Thus, in the fixed-anchor contraction
lemmas below, we write the estimates in the norms anchored at

y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
and
y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
, even though Algorithm
1

uses the frozen metrics
\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})
and
\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})
. All constants below are
understood after absorbing fixed
\eta
-dependent metric-comparison factors. We therefore relabel
the enlarged constants as

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta},\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}
, etc., without changing the schedule scaling or the
convergence rate.

E.2
Contraction for the
y
-tracker

Lemma 5

(Contraction of the proxy tracker)
.

Fix an outer iteration
k
. Suppose the proxy inner iterates remain in

T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
, and suppose

\alpha_{k}\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\leq\frac{1}{8},\qquad\beta_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\leq\frac{1}{8},\qquad\beta_{k}:=\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}.

(18)

Then

\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)^{T}\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(19)

Proof.

The implemented proxy-tracker update in Algorithm
1
uses the frozen barrier-metric

\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k})
during the
T
inner steps. By
Lemma
4
, on the maintained proxy neighborhood this frozen-metric
step admits the same anchored contraction normal form as a center-metric step, after absorbing fixed

\eta
-dependent comparison factors into

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta},\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}
. Thus it suffices to prove the contraction in the
anchored Dikin norm at
y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
.

Apply Lemma
4
with

h(y)=L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y),\qquad c=y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\qquad\bar{y}=y_{k},\qquad B=\nabla^{2}\phi(y_{k}).

By Proposition
1
and
Lemma
3
, for every

y\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
,

\frac{\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

(20)

Therefore,
y\mapsto L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y)
is

\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}/2
-strongly convex and

\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}
-smooth with respect to the anchored norm

\|\cdot\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}
on the maintained proxy neighborhood.

Thus, for every inner iterate

y_{k}^{(t)}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
, strong convexity gives

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\frac{\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(21)

Since
y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
minimizes
L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},\cdot)
,

\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))=0,

and hence (
21
) becomes

\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle\geq\frac{\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(22)

Moreover, convexity and smoothness imply the Baillon–Haddad co-coercivity inequality in the
anchored primal/dual pair:

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

(23)

Using again

\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))=0
, this gives

\|\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}\leq\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

(24)

By the anchored normal form supplied by
Lemma
4
, the one-step proxy update satisfies the
center-anchor expansion

\displaystyle\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\left\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})-\alpha_{k}\bigl(H_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})\right\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-2\alpha_{k}\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}.

Here the expansion is written in the center-anchor norm for analysis; the implemented frozen-metric
step is reduced to this normal form by Lemma
4
.

Applying (
24
) to the last term gives

\alpha_{k}^{2}\|\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}\leq\alpha_{k}^{2}\bigl(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\Bigl(2\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{2}(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta})\Bigr)\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

Under the step-size rule (
18
),

\alpha_{k}(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta})=\beta_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\alpha_{k}\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

Thus

2\alpha_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{2}(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta})=\alpha_{k}\Bigl(2-\alpha_{k}(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}+\ell_{f,1}^{\eta})\Bigr)\geq\frac{7}{4}\alpha_{k}.

Combining this with (
22
), we get

\displaystyle\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\frac{7}{4}\alpha_{k}\left\langle\nabla_{y}L_{\lambda_{k},\mu}(x_{k},y_{k}^{(t)}),\,y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(1-\frac{7}{8}\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\right)\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\left(1-\frac{7}{8}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}\right)\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Therefore,

\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(25)

Repeating (
25
) for
t=0,\ldots,T-1
, and using

y_{k}^{(0)}=y_{k}
and
y_{k+1}=y_{k}^{(T)}
, gives

\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)^{T}\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

This proves (
19
).
∎

Corollary 1

(Scheduled fixed-anchor contraction of the proxy tracker)
.

Under the step size requirement of Lemma
5
, if in addition

T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}\leq 1/4
, then

\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{4}\beta_{k}\right)\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Proof.

The proof of Lemma
5
gives the one-step bound

\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2},

because
\alpha_{k}\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\beta_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\leq 1/4
. Repeating this
T
times
and using
T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}\leq 1/4
yields

\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)^{T}\leq 1-\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{4}\beta_{k},

which proves the claim.
∎

E.3
Dikin Recursion for the
y
-tracker

Lemma 6

(Moving-anchor recursion for the proxy tracker)
.

Fix an outer iteration
k
and define

\delta_{k}:=\lambda_{k+1}-\lambda_{k}\geq 0
. Let

I_{k+1}:=\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})}^{2}.

Then, for any
j_{k}>0
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{k+1}I_{k+1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\lambda_{k}\Bigg[\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{k+1}}{\lambda_{k}}\Big(1+\frac{2\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{k+1}\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[2(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}+\frac{(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}}+\frac{1}{2}\Bigg]\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[\frac{8(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k+1}}+2\frac{\kappa_{y,k}^{2}(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\lambda_{k+1}}{\lambda_{k}}\Bigg]\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}.

(26)

Proof.

Anchor change.

Define

\kappa_{y,k}:=\left(1-\big\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\right)^{-1}.

Whenever the center displacement is less than one in the old anchor norm, Lemma
1

applied to the two proxy anchors gives

\|u\|_{y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})}^{2}\leq\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\|u\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\qquad\forall u.

Therefore,

I_{k+1}\leq\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(27)

Expanding the squared anchored Dikin norm at the old proxy anchor gives

\displaystyle\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+\big\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-2\Big\langle y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\,y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\Big\rangle_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}.

(28)

Center drift.

Apply Proposition
2
(i) with

(\lambda_{1},x_{1})=(\lambda_{k},x_{k})
and

(\lambda_{2},x_{2})=(\lambda_{k+1},x_{k+1})
. This gives

\big\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{2\delta_{k}\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\lambda_{k}\lambda_{k+1}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}+\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}.

(29)

Squaring and using
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
yields

\displaystyle\big\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\;

\displaystyle\frac{8(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}\lambda_{k+1}^{2}}+2(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}^{2}.

(30)

Since
x_{k+1}-x_{k}=-\xi\alpha_{k}q_{k}^{x}
, we have

\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}^{2}=\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

Cross term.

Consider the cross term in (
28
). Because
Lemma
14
is stated in the anchored Dikin geometry at

y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
, and its right-hand side depends on the test vector only through
anchored Dikin norms, the same bound applies after replacing the test vector by its negative. Hence,
for any
j_{k}>0
, applying Lemma
14
with

v_{k}:=y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})

gives

\displaystyle-2\Big\langle y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\,y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\Big\rangle_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Bigg(\frac{2\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigg)\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2j_{k}}(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\right)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}+2\frac{\kappa_{y,k}^{2}(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{3}}.

(31)

Collecting terms.

Combining (
27
), (
28
),
(
30
), and (
31
), we obtain

\displaystyle I_{k+1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg(1+\frac{2\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[2(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{2}\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{j_{k}}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\Bigg]\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[\frac{8(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}\lambda_{k+1}^{2}}+2\frac{\kappa_{y,k}^{2}(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{3}}\Bigg].

(32)

Multiplying (
32
) by
\lambda_{k+1}
and rearranging the
last line gives (
26
).
∎

Corollary 2

(Scheduled moving-anchor recursion for the proxy tracker)
.

Adopting the convention from
Appendix
J
that
\ell_{*,1}^{\eta}
is enlarged so that

\ell_{*,1}^{\eta}\geq\max\{1,\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta}\}
, if

\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\leq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\beta_{k},\qquad 2\xi^{2}\max\{\ell_{g,0}^{2},\ell_{f,0}^{2}\}(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\beta_{k},

(33)

then

I_{k+1}\leq\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\left(1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}+\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\right)\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+O\!\left(\frac{\xi^{2}(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}T\beta_{k}}\right)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}+O\!\left(\frac{(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{3}}\right).

(34)

Proof.

Choose

j_{k}=\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\lambda_{k}}{16\xi}.

Then

2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}=\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}.

Substituting this choice into (
32
) gives

\displaystyle I_{k+1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg(1+\frac{2\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}+\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}+(\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\cdot\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[2(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{8(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}T\beta_{k}}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\Bigg]\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\Bigg[\frac{8(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}\lambda_{k+1}^{2}}+2\frac{\kappa_{y,k}^{2}(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{3}}\Bigg].

(35)

We now bound the parenthesis in the first line of (
35
).
Split
2\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}=\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}+\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}
and bound the second
copy by the first inequality in (
33
), giving

\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}\leq T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/16
. The contraction-budget term is

T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/8
. For the anchor-switch term, the convention

\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta}\leq\ell_{*,1}^{\eta}
and
\alpha_{k}\leq\beta_{k}
(using

\lambda_{k}\geq 1
) give

(\ell_{\lambda,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\leq 5\xi^{2}(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\max\{\ell_{g,0}^{2},\ell_{f,0}^{2}\}\beta_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{5T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{32}\beta_{k},

where the last step uses the second inequality in (
33
).
Summing the four pieces yields

\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}+T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/16+T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/8+5T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/32=\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}+11T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/32\leq\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}+3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/8
, i.e.

\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}.

Thus the first line of (
35
) is bounded by

\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\left(1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}+\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\right)\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

The second line is absorbed into

O\!\left(\frac{\xi^{2}(\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta})^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}T\beta_{k}}\right)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2},

after increasing the absolute constant if necessary. The last line is bounded by

O\!\left(\frac{(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{2}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{3}}\right),

using
\lambda_{k+1}\geq\lambda_{k}
and the schedule condition

\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}=O(\beta_{k})
. Combining these bounds proves
(
34
).
∎

E.4
Contraction for the
z
-tracker

We next analyze the inner tracker
z_{k}
, whose role is to follow the moving exact lower
minimizer
y_{\mu}^{*}(x_{k})
. As on the proxy side, the analysis separates into a fixed-anchor
contraction and a moving-anchor recursion.

Lemma 7

(Contraction of the exact tracker)
.

Fix an outer iteration
k
. Suppose the exact inner iterates remain in

T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, and suppose

\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\,\gamma_{k}\leq\frac{1}{4},\qquad T\,\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\,\gamma_{k}\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(36)

Then

\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\bigl(1-\gamma_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\bigr)^{T}\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(37)

Proof.

The implemented exact-tracker update in Algorithm
1
uses the frozen barrier
metric
\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k})
during the
T
inner steps. By
Lemma
4
, on the maintained exact neighborhood this
frozen-metric step admits the same anchored contraction normal form as a center-metric step, after
absorbing fixed
\eta
-dependent comparison factors into

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
and
\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}
. Thus it suffices to carry out the contraction argument in
the anchored Dikin norm at
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
.

Apply Lemma
4
with

h(y)=\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y),\qquad c=y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\qquad\bar{y}=z_{k},\qquad B=\nabla^{2}\phi(z_{k}).

By Proposition
1
, for every

y\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
,

\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\preceq\nabla_{yy}^{2}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y)\preceq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}).

(38)

Therefore,
y\mapsto\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y)
is
\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
-strongly convex and

\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}
-smooth with respect to the anchored norm

\|\cdot\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}
on the maintained exact neighborhood.

Thus, for every inner iterate
z_{k}^{(t)}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, strong convexity gives

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(39)

Since
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
minimizes
\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},\cdot)
,

\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))=0,

and hence (
39
) becomes

\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)}),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle\geq\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(40)

Moreover, convexity and smoothness imply the Baillon–Haddad co-coercivity inequality in the
anchored primal/dual pair:

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})-\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

(41)

Using again
\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))=0
, this gives

\|\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)}),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

(42)

By the anchored normal form supplied by
Lemma
4
, the one-step exact-tracker update satisfies the
center-anchor expansion

\displaystyle\|z_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\left\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})-\gamma_{k}\bigl(H_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\bigr)^{-1}\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})\right\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-2\gamma_{k}\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)}),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\gamma_{k}^{2}\|\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}.

Here the expansion is written in the exact center-anchor norm for analysis; the implemented frozen
barrier-metric step is reduced to this normal form by
Lemma
4
.

Applying (
42
) to the last term yields

\gamma_{k}^{2}\|\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),*}^{2}\leq\gamma_{k}^{2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)}),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|z_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\bigl(2\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{k}^{2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\bigr)\left\langle\nabla_{y}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k}^{(t)}),\,z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\right\rangle.

Under the step-size condition (
36
),

2\gamma_{k}-\gamma_{k}^{2}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}=\gamma_{k}(2-\gamma_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})\geq\gamma_{k}.

Combining this with (
40
), we obtain the one-step contraction

\displaystyle\|z_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\gamma_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigl(1-\gamma_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\bigr)\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(43)

Repeating (
43
) for
t=0,\ldots,T-1
, and using

z_{k}^{(0)}=z_{k}
and
z_{k+1}=z_{k}^{(T)}
, gives

\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\bigl(1-\gamma_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\bigr)^{T}\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

This proves (
37
).
∎

Corollary 3

(Scheduled fixed-anchor contraction of the
z
-tracker)
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
7
, if in addition

T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}\leq 1/4
, then

\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{4}\gamma_{k}\right)\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Proof.

Lemma
7
gives

\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k})^{T}\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Since
T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}\leq 1/4
, the elementary bound

(1-a)^{T}\leq 1-\frac{3}{4}Ta
for
Ta\leq 1/4
, with
a=\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}
, yields the claim.
∎

E.5
Dikin Recursion for the
z
-tracker

Lemma 8

(Moving-anchor recursion for the exact tracker)
.

Fix an outer iteration
k
. Define

J_{k+1}:=\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})}^{2}.

Let

\kappa_{z,k}:=\left(1-\big\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\right)^{-1}.

Then, for any
j_{k}>0
,

\displaystyle J_{k+1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\Bigl(1+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigr)\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\Bigl((\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2j_{k}}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\Bigr)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

(44)

Proof.

Anchor change.

Whenever

\big\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}<1,

Lemma
1
, applied to the two exact anchors, gives

\|u\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})}^{2}\leq\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\|u\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\qquad\forall u.

Hence

J_{k+1}=\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})}^{2}\leq\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

(45)

Expanding the squared norm at the old anchor
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-2\Big\langle z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\Big\rangle_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}.

(46)

Center drift.

By Proposition
2
(ii),

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}.

Using the outer update
x_{k+1}-x_{k}=-\xi\alpha_{k}q_{k}^{x}
, this gives

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

(47)

Cross term.

Consider the cross term in (
46
). Lemma
13
is
stated in the anchored Dikin geometry at
y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
, and its right-hand side depends on
the test vector only through anchored Dikin norms. Therefore the same bound applies after replacing
the test vector by its negative. Taking

v_{k}:=z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),

we obtain, for any
j_{k}>0
,

\displaystyle-2\Big\langle z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\,y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\Big\rangle_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\Bigl(2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigr)\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2j_{k}}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\right)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

(48)

Collecting terms.

Combining (
45
), (
46
),
(
47
), and (
48
), we get

\displaystyle J_{k+1}\leq\;

\displaystyle\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\Bigl(1+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\Bigr)\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\Bigl((\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2j_{k}}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}\Bigr)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

This proves (
44
).
∎

Corollary 4

(Scheduled moving-anchor recursion for the exact tracker)
.

If

(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\leq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\gamma_{k},

(49)

then

J_{k+1}\leq\kappa_{z,k}^{2}\left(1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\gamma_{k}\right)\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+O\!\left(\frac{\xi^{2}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}\right)\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

(50)

Proof.

Choose

j_{k}:=\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}{16\xi\alpha_{k}}.

Then

2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}=\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\gamma_{k}.

Substituting this choice into (
44
), and using
(
49
), gives

1+2\xi\alpha_{k}j_{k}+(\ell_{*,1}^{\eta})^{2}\xi^{2}\bigl(\ell_{f,0}^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}+4\ell_{g,0}^{2}\beta_{k}^{2}\bigr)\leq 1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\gamma_{k}\leq 1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\gamma_{k}.

Moreover,

\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2j_{k}}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}=\frac{8\xi^{2}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}.

The remaining terms in the coefficient of
\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}
are absorbed into the same order after
increasing the absolute constant. Therefore (
50
) follows.
∎

Appendix F
Tube Invariance

All local estimates above are valid on the enlarged buffer neighborhood. We now show that the

barrier-aware
schedule keeps the algorithmic iterates inside the smaller target neighborhoods. Define the

T
-step contraction gaps

\Gamma_{\psi,k}:=1-\bigl(1-\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}\bigr)^{T},\qquad\Gamma_{L,k}:=1-\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)^{T},\qquad\beta_{k}:=\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}.

(51)

The tube-maintenance mechanism is that fixed-center contraction creates the gaps

\Gamma_{\psi,k}
and
\Gamma_{L,k}
, while the center drifts

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}
and
\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}
consume part of these gaps.

Lemma 9

(Explicit drift bounds)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds, the local consequences in
Proposition
2
hold, and the multiplier schedule satisfies

\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\leq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\beta_{k},\qquad\delta_{k}:=\lambda_{k+1}-\lambda_{k}\geq 0,\qquad\lambda_{k}\geq\lambda_{0}.

Then

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\xi\,\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)\beta_{k},

(52)

and

\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\left(\frac{T\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{8\lambda_{0}}+\xi\,\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)\right)\beta_{k}.

(53)

Proof.

First, by the definition of
q_{k}^{x}
, the identity
\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}=\nabla_{x}g
, and the coarse
gradient bounds,

\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}\leq\ell_{f,0}+2\lambda_{k}\ell_{g,0}\leq\lambda_{k}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right).

Since
x_{k+1}=x_{k}-\xi\alpha_{k}q_{k}^{x}
, this gives

\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}\leq\xi\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)=\xi\beta_{k}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right).

The exact-center bound follows from Proposition
2
(ii):

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2},

which proves (
52
).

For the proxy center, Proposition
2
(i), applied with

(\lambda_{1},x_{1})=(\lambda_{k},x_{k})
and

(\lambda_{2},x_{2})=(\lambda_{k+1},x_{k+1})
, gives

\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{2\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}\lambda_{k+1}}+\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}.

Using
\lambda_{k+1}\geq\lambda_{0}
and

\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}\leq T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/16
, the first term is bounded by

\frac{2\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}\lambda_{k+1}}\leq\frac{2\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{16\lambda_{0}}=\frac{T\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{8\lambda_{0}}\beta_{k}.

Combining this with the bound on
\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}
proves
(
53
).
∎

F.1
Closed-form Sufficient Conditions for the Drift Bounds

Corollary 5

(
barrier-aware
schedules imply drift closure)
.

Suppose the schedule satisfies
\beta_{k}\leq\gamma_{k}
,

T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}\leq 1
,
T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}\leq 1
, and the multiplier-growth
condition in Definition
1
. If

\frac{\xi}{T}\leq\frac{\eta\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\left(\ell_{f,0}/\lambda_{0}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)},\qquad\frac{\xi}{T}\leq\frac{\eta\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{64\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\left(\ell_{f,0}/\lambda_{0}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)},

(54)

and

\lambda_{0}\geq\frac{8\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{\eta\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}},

(55)

then the center drifts satisfy

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{\psi,k},\qquad\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{L,k}.

(56)

In particular, the first two terms in the tube-closure condition of
Definition
1
(
S3
), together with the lower bound on
\lambda_{0}
, imply the
abstract drift conditions required for tube maintenance.

Proof.

Since
T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}\leq 1
, the elementary bound

1-(1-a)^{T}\geq Ta/2
for
Ta\leq 1
gives

\Gamma_{\psi,k}=1-(1-\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k})^{T}\geq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{2}\gamma_{k}.

Similarly, since
T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}\leq 1
,

\Gamma_{L,k}=1-\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)^{T}\geq\frac{7T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\beta_{k}\geq\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{4}\beta_{k}.

For the exact center, Lemma
9
and
\beta_{k}\leq\gamma_{k}
give

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\xi\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)\gamma_{k}.

The first inequality in (
54
) implies

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{16}\gamma_{k}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{\psi,k}.

For the proxy center, Lemma
9
gives

\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\left(\frac{T\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{8\lambda_{0}}+\xi\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)\right)\beta_{k}.

The lower bound (
55
) implies

\frac{T\ell_{f,0}^{\eta}}{8\lambda_{0}}\leq\frac{\eta T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{64},

and the second inequality in (
54
) implies

\xi\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\lambda_{0}}+2\ell_{g,0}\right)\leq\frac{\eta T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{64}.

Therefore

\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{32}\beta_{k}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{L,k}.

This proves (
56
).
∎

Remark 4
.

Corollary
5
is the explicit closure calculation behind the first two terms
of the tube-closure condition in Definition
1
(
S3
). The remaining term in
Definition
1
(
S3
) controls the higher-order anchor-switch factors appearing in the
moving-anchor recursions of Appendix
E
. Thus the full barrier-aware schedule
contains exactly the ingredients needed for both tube maintenance and the later Lyapunov descent.

F.2
Proof of Theorem
2

We first record a deterministic neighborhood-transition lemma.

Lemma 10

(Contraction plus drift implies neighborhood transition)
.

Let
\bar{y},\bar{y}^{+}\in\operatorname{int}(Y)
, let
\eta\in(0,1/2)
, and let

\Gamma\in[0,1]
. Suppose

\|\bar{y}^{+}-\bar{y}\|_{\bar{y}}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma.

If
u\in\operatorname{int}(Y)
satisfies

\|u-\bar{y}\|_{\bar{y}}\leq\eta\sqrt{1-\Gamma},

then

\|u-\bar{y}^{+}\|_{\bar{y}^{+}}\leq\eta.

Proof.

Let
d:=\|\bar{y}^{+}-\bar{y}\|_{\bar{y}}
. Since
d\leq\eta\Gamma/8<1
, the anchor-switch bound gives

\|v\|_{\bar{y}^{+}}\leq(1-d)^{-1}\|v\|_{\bar{y}}\qquad\forall v.

Therefore

\|u-\bar{y}^{+}\|_{\bar{y}^{+}}\leq(1-d)^{-1}\bigl(\|u-\bar{y}\|_{\bar{y}}+\|\bar{y}^{+}-\bar{y}\|_{\bar{y}}\bigr).

Using the assumptions,

\|u-\bar{y}^{+}\|_{\bar{y}^{+}}\leq\eta\frac{\sqrt{1-\Gamma}+\Gamma/8}{1-\eta\Gamma/8}.

Since
\sqrt{1-\Gamma}\leq 1-\Gamma/2
for
\Gamma\in[0,1]
and
\eta<1/2
,

\sqrt{1-\Gamma}+\frac{\Gamma}{8}\leq 1-\frac{3\Gamma}{8}\leq 1-\frac{\eta\Gamma}{8}.

Thus
\|u-\bar{y}^{+}\|_{\bar{y}^{+}}\leq\eta
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
2

Proof.

The proof is by induction on the outer iteration
k
. The base case follows from the assumed
initialization

z_{0}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{0}),\qquad y_{0}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{0}}(x_{0}).

Assume that, at the beginning of outer iteration
k
,

z_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k}),\qquad y_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k}).

We prove that all inner iterates remain in the corresponding target neighborhoods and that the next warm
starts lie in the target neighborhoods at
x_{k+1}
and
\lambda_{k+1}
.

First consider the exact tracker. Since
z_{k}^{(0)}=z_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, and

T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})\subseteq T_{2\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, the local Dikin regularity estimates are valid at

z_{k}^{(0)}
. The one-step contraction estimate from Lemma
7
gives, whenever

z_{k}^{(t)}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
,

\|z_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k})\|z_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Thus, by induction over
t=0,\ldots,T-1
, every exact inner iterate remains in

T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
. After
T
inner steps,

\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\Gamma_{\psi,k})\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\Gamma_{\psi,k})\eta^{2}.

By Corollary
5
,

\|y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{\psi,k}.

Applying Lemma
10
with

\bar{y}=y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\qquad\bar{y}^{+}=y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1}),\qquad u=z_{k+1},\qquad\Gamma=\Gamma_{\psi,k},

we obtain

z_{k+1}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k+1}).

The proxy tracker is handled in the same way. Since

y_{k}^{(0)}=y_{k}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
, the local proxy neighborhood estimates are valid at the
initial proxy inner iterate. The one-step contraction estimate from Lemma
5

implies that whenever
y_{k}^{(t)}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
,

\|y_{k}^{(t+1)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{7\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{8}\beta_{k}\right)\|y_{k}^{(t)}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}.

Hence all proxy inner iterates remain in
T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
, and after
T
steps,

\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\Gamma_{L,k})\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\leq(1-\Gamma_{L,k})\eta^{2}.

By Corollary
5
,

\|y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1})-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}\leq\frac{\eta}{8}\Gamma_{L,k}.

Applying Lemma
10
with

\bar{y}=y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}),\qquad\bar{y}^{+}=y_{\lambda_{k+1},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k+1}),\qquad u=y_{k+1},\qquad\Gamma=\Gamma_{L,k},

we obtain

y_{k+1}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k+1}}(x_{k+1}).

Therefore the induction hypothesis propagates from
k
to
k+1
. By induction, for every outer
iteration
k
, all exact inner iterates remain in
T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k})
, all proxy inner iterates
remain in
T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k}}(x_{k})
, and the next warm starts satisfy

z_{k+1}\in T_{\eta,\mu}(x_{k+1}),\qquad y_{k+1}\in T_{\eta,\mu}^{\lambda_{k+1}}(x_{k+1}).

This proves forward invariance of the exact and proxy target neighborhoods.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of Theorem
3

G.1
One-step Descent Decomposition

We now combine the two ingredients established in the previous appendices.
Appendix
D
shows that, inside the maintained Dikin neighborhoods, the barrier-smoothed
outer objective
F_{\mu}
is locally smooth and the proxy-gradient bias is
O(1/\lambda)
.
Appendix
E
then quantifies how the two inner trackers evolve in anchored Dikin
norms, producing fixed-center contraction and moving-anchor recursions for the proxy and exact
tracking errors.

The purpose of this subsection is to merge these facts into a one-step descent estimate for the outer
objective, and then for a Lyapunov function coupling
F_{\mu}
with the two tracker errors. The first
result expresses the one-step decrease of
F_{\mu}(x_{k})
in terms of the current-center proxy and
exact tracking errors, together with the
O(1/\lambda_{k})
proxy bias. These current-center terms are
then handled by the tracker recursions from Appendix
E
.

Proposition 4

(Outer descent under current-center tracker errors)
.

Fix an outer iterate
x_{k}\in X
and suppose

\xi L_{F}^{\eta}\alpha_{k}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

(57)

Then, for each
k
,

\displaystyle F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})-F_{\mu}(x_{k})

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{4}\Bigl(2\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}^{2}+\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\Bigl[(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})^{2}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})^{2}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+\frac{(c_{x}^{\eta})^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}\Bigr].

(58)

Here
c_{x}^{\eta}
is the local Dikin constant from the proxy-gradient bias estimate in
Proposition
2
(iv)
.

Moreover, if

\lambda_{k}\geq\lambda_{0}\geq\frac{2\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}},\qquad\frac{\xi}{T}\leq c_{\xi}\frac{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}}{(\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})^{2}},

(59)

for a sufficiently small absolute constant
c_{\xi}>0
, then

\displaystyle F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})-F_{\mu}(x_{k})

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{4}\Bigl(2\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}^{2}+\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}^{2}}{32}\Bigl(2\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\frac{(c_{x}^{\eta})^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}.

(60)

Proof.

By
L_{F}^{\eta}
-smoothness of
F_{\mu}
on
X
,

F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})\leq F_{\mu}(x_{k})+\langle\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k}),x_{k+1}-x_{k}\rangle+\frac{L_{F}^{\eta}}{2}\|x_{k+1}-x_{k}\|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting the outer update
x_{k+1}-x_{k}=-\xi\alpha_{k}q_{k}^{x}
and using
(
57
), we get

F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})-F_{\mu}(x_{k})\leq-\xi\alpha_{k}\langle\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k}),q_{k}^{x}\rangle+\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{4}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

Using

\langle a,b\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\bigl(\|a\|_{2}^{2}+\|b\|_{2}^{2}-\|a-b\|_{2}^{2}\bigr),

we obtain

F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})-F_{\mu}(x_{k})\leq-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{4}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})-q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}.

(61)

We now bound the proxy-direction error. Add and subtract

\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})
:

\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})-q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}\leq\|q_{k}^{x}-\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\|_{2}+\|\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})-\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}.

By Proposition
2
(iv),

\|\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})-\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}\leq\frac{c_{x}^{\eta}}{\lambda_{k}}.

(62)

For the first term, the envelope identity for
C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}
gives

\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})=\nabla_{x}f(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))+\lambda_{k}\Bigl(\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\Bigr).

Therefore,

\displaystyle q_{k}^{x}-\nabla C_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{x}f(x_{k},y_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}f(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{k}\Bigl(\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad-\lambda_{k}\Bigl(\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\Bigr).

Using the local cross-derivative bounds for
f
and
\psi_{\mu}
on the maintained proxy and
exact neighborhoods,

\|\nabla_{x}f(x_{k},y_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}f(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\|_{2}\leq\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})},

\|\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\|_{2}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})},

and

\|\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},z_{k+1})-\nabla_{x}\psi_{\mu}(x_{k},y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k}))\|_{2}\leq\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}.

Combining these bounds with (
62
) gives

\displaystyle\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})-q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}+\frac{c_{x}^{\eta}}{\lambda_{k}}.

Squaring and using
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})-q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\Bigl[(\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})^{2}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+(\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta})^{2}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}+\frac{(c_{x}^{\eta})^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}\Bigr].

(63)

Plugging (
63
) into (
61
) proves
(
58
).

It remains to derive the simplified current-center form. Since

\lambda_{k}\geq\lambda_{0}\geq 2\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}/\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
and

\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\geq\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}
, we have

\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}\leq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{k}\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\leq\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta},

and hence

\ell_{f,1}^{\eta}+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\leq\frac{3}{2}\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}.

By choosing
c_{\xi}
in (
59
) sufficiently small, the two tracker
error coefficients in (
58
) are bounded by

\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}^{2}}{16}\quad\text{for the proxy error,}\qquad\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\alpha_{k}\lambda_{k}^{2}}{32}\quad\text{for the exact error.}

Therefore (
58
) reduces to
(
60
).
∎

G.2
Lyapunov Descent

The outer descent estimate in Proposition
4
contains positive terms involving
the current-center proxy and exact tracking errors. The moving-anchor recursions in
Appendix
E
show that these terms can be absorbed by contraction of the warm-start
errors
I_{k}
and
J_{k}
, provided the schedule controls center drift and anchor-switch effects. We
therefore introduce a Lyapunov function coupling
F_{\mu}(x_{k})
with the two anchored tracker
errors. The resulting inequality is the main deterministic descent step: it leaves a negative gradient
term, negative tracker-error terms, and only summable
O(\alpha_{k}/\lambda_{k}^{2})
and

O(\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}^{2})
remainders.

Proposition 5

(Lyapunov descent)
.

Fix a neighborhood radius
\eta\in(0,1/2)
and an inner-loop length
T\in\mathbb{N}
. Suppose the
barrier-aware conditions in Definition
1
hold, and the iterates remain in the
target neighborhoods guaranteed by Theorem
2
. Define the tracker errors

I_{k}:=\big\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2},\qquad J_{k}:=\big\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2},

(64)

and the Lyapunov function

V_{k}:=F_{\mu}(x_{k})+\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}I_{k}+\frac{\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{2}J_{k}.

(65)

Then, for each
k
,

\displaystyle V_{k+1}-V_{k}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{8}\,\big\|y_{k}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}{16}\,\big\|z_{k}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+O\!\left(\xi(c_{x}^{\eta})^{2}\right)\frac{\alpha_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+O\!\left(\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{3}}\right)\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}.

(66)

Proof.

From the definition of
V_{k}
,

\displaystyle V_{k+1}-V_{k}

\displaystyle=F_{\mu}(x_{k+1})-F_{\mu}(x_{k})+\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\bigl(\lambda_{k+1}I_{k+1}-\lambda_{k}I_{k}\bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{2}\bigl(\lambda_{k+1}J_{k+1}-\lambda_{k}J_{k}\bigr).

(67)

We use Proposition
4
in its balanced current-center form. Namely, after the
outer descent estimate and the
barrier-aware
step-size bounds, the current-center tracking errors enter
with budgets

\frac{\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{16}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

for the proxy tracker, and

\frac{\lambda_{k}\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}{64}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}

for the exact tracker. The latter uses
\beta_{k}\leq\gamma_{k}
. Substituting this into
(
67
) gives

\displaystyle V_{k+1}-V_{k}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\|\nabla F_{\mu}(x_{k})\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{\xi\alpha_{k}}{4}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{3\xi\alpha_{k}}{2}\frac{(c_{x}^{\eta})^{2}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}\underbrace{\Bigl(\lambda_{k+1}I_{k+1}+\frac{\lambda_{k}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{16}\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\lambda_{k}I_{k}\Bigr)}_{(i)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\ell_{\psi,1}^{\eta}}{2}\underbrace{\Bigl(\lambda_{k+1}J_{k+1}+\frac{\lambda_{k}T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\gamma_{k}}{32}\big\|z_{k+1}-y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\lambda_{k}J_{k}\Bigr)}_{(ii)}.

(68)

We first control the proxy block
(i)
. By
Corollary
2
,

\displaystyle(i)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{k}\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\left(1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{8}+\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\right)\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\lambda_{k}I_{k}+(iii),

(69)

where

(iii):=O\!\left(\xi^{2}(\ell_{*,0}^{\eta})^{2}\right)\frac{\lambda_{k}\alpha_{k}^{2}}{\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}T\beta_{k}}\|q_{k}^{x}\|_{2}^{2}+O\!\left(\frac{(\ell_{f,0}^{\eta})^{2}}{(\rho_{\psi}^{\eta})^{3}}\right)\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}.

(70)

Here, as usual,
\ell_{*,0}^{\eta}
may be enlarged to dominate the proxy center-stability constant

\ell_{\lambda,0}^{\eta}
.

The multiplier-growth condition
\delta_{k}/\lambda_{k}\leq T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}/16
implies

1+\frac{3T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{8}+\frac{\delta_{k}}{\lambda_{k}}\leq 1+\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{2}.

Therefore

\displaystyle(i)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{k}\kappa_{y,k}^{2}\left(1+\frac{T\rho_{\psi}^{\eta}\beta_{k}}{2}\right)\big\|y_{k+1}-y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})\big\|_{y_{\lambda_{k},\mu}^{\star}(x_{k})}^{2}-\lambda_{k}I_{k}+(iii).

(71)

By Corollary
1
,
